# Graph Report - hal0  (2026-07-19)

## Corpus Check
- 1475 files · ~1,795,072 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 26320 nodes · 50076 edges · 1202 communities (1082 shown, 120 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6024 edges (avg confidence: 0.74)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `cb162948`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
